--- Commit Message ---
LU-20211 mdt: trunc(0) layout replace on inactive stripes

When truncate(0) hits a file whose layout references deactivated or
disconnected OSTs, and the client advertises
OBD_CONNECT2_LAYOUT_REPLACE, replace the layout: move the old layout
onto a volatile victim and instantiate a fresh layout on the file from
the existing pattern via QoS/RR.  A full or read-only OST is still
usable and does not trigger this.  If the new layout cannot be
allocated, keep the old layout and do a normal trunc(0).

Skip replace when any component has LCME_FL_NOSYNC.  That flag freezes
the mirror's existing objects; swap would discard the snapshot.  The
NOSYNC test runs once, inside mdt_replace_unavailable_layout(), which
returns 0 so setattr falls through to a normal trunc(0).

mdt_reint_setattr() does not check NOSYNC.  It probes for an
unavailable layout without MDS_INODELOCK_LAYOUT so a healthy trunc(0)
skips LAYOUT EX.  What is re-checked under LAYOUT|XATTR EX is that
unavailable-stripe result, so a concurrent trunc(0) that already
replaced the layout does not allocate a second set of objects.

Extend the layout API with MD_LAYOUT_UNAVAILABLE (detect stripes on
offline OSTs) and MD_LAYOUT_INSTANTIATE (allocate from a source
pattern).  Teach lod_generate_lovea() to emit uninstantiated LOVEA
templates (as_template), omit placement hints so new stripes use
QoS/RR, and trigger replacement from mdt_reint_setattr() via
mdt_replace_unavailable_layout().

Layout-type handling for as_template / instantiate:
- DoM: never carries OST objects; force stripe_count to 0 and skip
  the OST object array so LOVEA size matches INIT DoM.
- PFL/DoM: keep the DoM component and replace only OST components.
- SEL: coerce LOV_MAGIC_SEL to COMP_V1 for lod_qos_parse_config();
  keep LCME_FL_EXTENSION and restamp SEL when LOVEA is written.
- EC: re-emit bound data/parity as unbound (IS_LINK_ID + shared
  link id) so lod_qos_prep_create() can pair before component IDs
  exist.

Layout replace creates a volatile victim via mdt_orphan_open().
Zero mti_spec first so that internal create does not inherit
leftover create state from the setattr thread.

On success, return MS_LAYOUT_REPLACED (with OBD_MD_FLEASIZE) in the
setattr reply.  The client then applies the new LOVEA via layout
change / prune.  By default prune uses CL_FSYNC_LOCAL and would
writeback dirty pages still cached against the old layout to the
unavailable OSTs (fail or hang).  When MS_LAYOUT_REPLACED is seen
with size 0, set coc_prune_mode to CL_FSYNC_DISCARD so prune discards
that stale OSC dirty cache locally instead of writeback; VFS page
cache is still dropped by ll_truncate_inode_pages_final() as usual.
The client reserves the default layout size in the trunc(0) setattr
reply buffer.  A resent trunc(0) setattr (lost or truncated reply)
returns the layout and MS_LAYOUT_REPLACED again.

Add sanity/test_27xt (plain, PFL/DoM, SEL), sanity-ec/test_75g (EC),
and sanity-flr/test_50e.

Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity serverversion=2.17 env=SANITY_EXCEPT="24D 27D"
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-flr serverversion=2.17 env=SANITY_FLR_EXCEPT=0a
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity clientversion=2.17

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Some concerns, see inline comments. Two of them look like real defects: the DoM change in lod_comp_shrink_stripe_count() alters the non-template LOVEA path and leaves uninitialised stripe slots on disk, and the layout replacement runs before setattr authorisation so a truncate that is later rejected still destroys the file. AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Mostly good, two small comments inline. One whole-patch thought: once a client connects with the new flag, every trunc(0)/open(O_TRUNC) on a file with a stripe on an inactive OST silently reallocates the layout and destroys the old OST objects, and there is no server or client tunable to turn that off. Would a mdt.*.enable_layout_replace (or equivalent) knob be worth adding so a site can fall back to the old behaviour without downgrading?
